Miten lähettää tämä kiinnostuneille sijoittajille, niin että he eivät voi kopioida tiedostoa ja se häviää kun on kerran tarkastellut?







Täydellisen "katsotaan vain kerran eikä voi kopsata" -suojauksen toteuttaminen digitaalisessa maailmassa on teknisesti mahdotonta (koska ruudulta voi aina ottaa valokuvan puhelimella), mutta pääomasijoittajien (VC) ja enkelisijoittajien kanssa toimittaessa suositellaan erilaista taktiikkaa.

Tässä on ensin paras tapa toimia sijoittajien kanssa, ja sen jälkeen työkalut, joilla saat teknisesti mahdollisimman tiukan suojauksen.

1. Parhaat käytännöt: Miten sijoittajien kanssa kannattaa toimia?

Suorat sanat alalta: Pääomasijoittajat eivät yleensä suostu allekirjoittamaan NDA-sopimuksia (salassapitosopimuksia) varhaisen vaiheen projekteissa, koska he näkevät satoja ideoita vuodessa. Jos asetat materiaalille liian tiukkoja esteitä (kuten "katsottavissa vain 1 minuutin ajan"), sijoittaja jättää esityksen (Deckin) kokonaan lukematta.

Suositeltu toimintatapa:

Teeripaperi / Teaser Deck (1–5 sivua): Lähetä sijoittajalle ensin vain korkean tason tiivistelmä (visio, markkina, tiimi, alustavat luvut). Älä laita tähän liikesalaisuuksia, tarkkoja algoritmeja tai lähdekoodia.

Kutsu tapaamiseen (Pitch): Kun sijoittaja kiinnostuu teaserista ja tapaatte (tai pidätte Zoom-callin), käytte läpi yksityiskohtaisen Playbookin / Deckin esittelemällä sen itse ruudunjajaon kautta.

Data Room (Syväluotaus): Jos sijoittaja etenemässä aikeissaan (Due Diligence -vaihe), hänelle annetaan pääsy suojattuun Data Roomiin (kuten DocSend), josta näkee kuka dokumenttia lukee.

2. Tekniset työkalut: Miten suojaat tiedoston käytännössä?

Jos haluat lähettää materiaalin siten, että sitä ei voi ladata, ei tulostaa, ja pääsy kumoutuu katselun jälkeen, käytä VDR-työkaluja (Virtual Data Room) tai spesifejä dokumenttien turvapalveluita.

Paras työkalu: DocSend (by Dropbox)

DocSend on startup-maailman standardi sijoittajamateriaalien lähettämiseen.

Ominaisuudet:

Voit kieltää lataamisen (No Download / Read-Only).

Voit asettaa linkille vanhenemispäivän tai rajoittaa katselukerrat (esim. max 1 katselukerta).

Voit vaatia sähköpostitunnistautumisen (vain tietty sijoittaja pääsee käsiksi).

Voit sulkea pääsyn linkkiin milloin tahansa (Revoke Access).

Näet sekunnin tarkkuudella, kuinka monta sekuntia sijoittaja vietti aikaa milläkin sivulla (esim. jos he hyppäsivät suoraan finanssisivulle).

Vaihtoehtoiset ilmaiset/halvemmat työkalut:

Digify: Mahdollistaa dokumenttien lähettämisen "Self-Destruct"-ominaisuudella (tiedosto tuhoutuu/hävää katselun jälkeen) sekä dynaamisella vesileimalla.

SendSafely / Papermine: Vastaavia suojattuja dokumenttien katseluympäristöjä.

Google Drive / PDF-suojaus (Perustaso): Voit jakaa PDF:n Google Drivesta katseluoikeudella ja ruksata asetuksista "Estä katselijoita lataamasta, tulostamasta ja kopioimasta". Tämä ei siis häviä automaattisesti, mutta estää suoran lataamisen.

3. Miten suojaat ideasi kopioinnilta? (Vesileimat)

Jos käytät DocSendiä tai Digifya, kytke päälle Dynaaminen vesileima (Dynamic Watermark).

Se tulostaa katselijan ruudulle (tiedoston päälle läpinäkyvästi) tekstin:

"CONFIDENTIAL – PREPARED FOR [SIJOITTAJAN_SÄHKÖPOSTI] – DO NOT DISTRIBUTE"

Tämä on tehokkain psykologinen este: vaikka sijoittaja ottaisi ruudusta valokuvan puhelimellaan, hänen oma sähköpostiosoitteensa / IP-osoitteensa näkyy kuvan päällä, jolloin hän ei voi jakaa sitä kilpailijoille jäämättä kiinni.

Saatekirje-esimerkki sijoittajalle

Kun lähetät suojatun DocSend-linkin, muotoile viesti ammattimaisesti:

Write brief highlights why and what are casinos pain points and how to solve those.







Here are the concise highlights of the core casino pain points and how GRNT APEX Terminal solves them:



1. Casino Pain Points (Why They Are Hurting)

Product Commoditization: Operators use the same B2B sportsbook odds providers (Kambi, SBTech, Altenar), making every casino look identical and reducing competition to margin-killing deposit bonuses.

Low Product Margins: Traditional single bets yield low house margins (4–6%), leaving casinos exposed to high volatility when favorites win.

Gen Z & Millennial Churn: Young, tech-savvy bettors find static 1X2 "Excel-style" bet slips boring—they want data, leverage, and dynamic UX like Robinhood or eToro, resulting in a 65% month-1 churn rate.

Short Session Times: Players place a quick bet in ~45 seconds and leave the app, missing out on cross-selling opportunities.

Extreme House Variance: Casinos bear 100% of the balance sheet risk when unexpected fight outcomes occur.

2. The Solution (How GRNT APEX Terminal Fixes It)

Fintech-Grade UX Overhaul: Replaces static bet slips with a dark-mode, Bloomberg-style terminal featuring 1x–20x leverage sliders, dynamic risk heatmaps, and eToro-style Copy-Trading.

Marginal Yield Boost (Parlay Nudge): Packages bets into visually framed "Quant Portfolios" (parlays), voluntarily pushing players toward high-margin bets (15–25% house edge).

Massive Session Engagement: Interactive scenario simulations and live feeds increase average session durations by +300% (from 45 seconds to 15+ minutes).

Automated Market Maker (AMM) Risk Shift: Moves balance sheet risk away from the casino to an x⋅y=k liquidity pool, generating risk-free continuous funding fees (0.75%) and liquidation spreads.

Zero Backend Friction: Integrates as a lightweight plug-and-play B2B iFrame/API layer—no complex database rebuilds, platform migrations, or operational risk.

Miksi tämä malli suojaa liiketoimintasalaisuutesi?

Yhdistetty "Volume Yield" (Solu B12 - 1.50 %):

Ulospäin näkyy: Yksinkertainen 1.50 % alustakomissio vivutetusta volyymista.

Sisäinen logiikka (Piilotettu): Tämä 1.50 % koostuu todellisuudessa kolmesta eri algoritmista: Continuous Funding Rate (0.75 %), Liquidation Spread Penalty (0.50 %) ja Dynamic Spread Expansion (0.25 %). Sijoittaja näkee tuoton, mutta ei laskentamoottoria.

Liukuva Volyymikerroin (Solu B6 - 3.5x):

Sijoittaja voi testata, mitä tapahtuu jos vivutusaste on 2x tai 5x. Kaavat laskevat tuloksen ilman, että Margin Call -kaavoja (Lp

​) tai AMM-hyperbeliyhtälöitä (x⋅y=k) paljastetaan.

Optimoidut Salkut (Solu B9 - 16.5 %):

Esittää kasinon marginaalin toteutuneena tuottoprosenttina. Se ei paljasta "Smart Money Nudge" -algoritmin painotuskertoimia tai Counter-Portfolio Internalization -logiikkaa.

Valmiit Skenaarioesimerkit (Syötä B4-soluun kokeillaksesi):

Skenaario 1 (Pieni Pilotti - 5 000 pelaajaa):

B4 = 5000 ⟹ GRNT Kuukausitulo: 136 450 € / kk (ARR: ~1.6M€)

Skenaario 2 (Keskikokoinen Scalaus - 25 000 pelaajaa):

B4 = 25000 ⟹ GRNT Kuukausitulo: 652 250 € / kk (ARR: ~7.8M€)

Skenaario 3 (Dominaatio / Enterprise - 50 000 pelaajaa):

B4 = 50000 ⟹ GRNT Kuukausitulo: 1 297 000 € / kk (ARR: ~15.5M€)

Miten tämä suojaa bisneslogiikkasi?

Volume Yield (1.50 %): Esitetään yhtenäisenä alustakomissiona. Todellisuudessa tämä 1.50 % muodostuu kolmesta erillisestä taustamekanismista (Funding Rate 0.75% + Liquidation Penalty 0.50% + Dynamic Spread 0.25%). Sijoittaja näkee tuoton, mutta ei järjestelmän matemaattista moottoria.

Dynamic Parlay Margin (16.5 %): Näkyy toteutuneena tuottoprosenttina ilman Smart Money Nudge -algoritmin tai Counter-Portfolio -sisäistämisen koodirakennetta.

Executive Summary: The B2B Operator Value Proposition

The Problem: Traditional sportsbooks suffer from severe product commoditization (identical odds suppliers, static 1X2 bet slips), reducing competition to margin-destroying deposit bonuses and high customer churn (65%+ month-1 drop-off).

The Solution: GRNT APEX Terminal installs as a lightweight, plug-and-play UI layer over your existing sportsbook backend. It shifts your audience from passive gamblers to active "Quant Traders," driving higher margins, longer session times, and zero balance-sheet risk via AMM liquidity pools.

│ THE CASINO PROFITABILITY TRINITY │

├───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤

│ 1. HIGHER MARGINS │ Parlay Nudge shifts single bets (5%) to parlays (16.5%). │

│ 2. HIGHER VOLUME │ 1x-20x leverage multiplies €100 collateral into €700 vol. │

│ 3. ZERO HOUSE RISK │ AMM Liquidity Pool absorbs player wins/losses completely. │

Volume Multiplication via Leverage: A player depositing €100 who utilizes an average leverage of 3.5x turns into €700 in traded volume. Casino commissions (NGR, funding rates, spreads) are collected on the full €700 volume, not the €100 deposit.

Behavioral Shift (Gambler → Quant): When players view betting through the lens of leverage, risk heatmaps, and expected value (EV+), their fear of multi-leg parlays decreases. They perceive multi-outcome portfolios as structured trades rather than wild gambles.

Continuous Yield Recapture: Traditional sportsbooks only make money when a event ends. GRNT APEX Terminal earns second-by-second funding fees on open leveraged positions and captures liquidation spreads when high-risk trades hit margin call thresholds. -> Create FULL business plan, winning strategy, scenarios, DCF, dev roadmap, list of "issues" of casinos and how GRNT solves those and generates huge profit to casino. 6. Critical Risk Factors & Mitigation Strategy
Regulatory Friction (MGA / UKGC Compliance):

Risk: Regulators blocking third-party external liquidity pooling.  
ODT

Mitigation: Switchable Dual-Mode AMM. Mode A runs GRNT-hosted pools for light-touch/crypto markets. Mode B deploys an isolated AMM vault inside the casino's own licensed legal entity, ensuring 100% local compliance while retaining zero balance-sheet risk.  
ODT
+ 2

Intellectual Property Theft / Cloning:

Risk: Operators or competitors copying the front-end leverage UI.  
ODT

Mitigation: Black-Box API Architecture. Operators receive a compiled React SDK/iFrame component. The core Python Monte Carlo heuristics, AMM bonding curves (x⋅y=k), and database logic run exclusively on GRNT's private cloud servers.  
ODT
+ 4

Liquidity Squeezes on High-Leverage Wins:

Risk: Massive wins draining the pool on 20x leveraged positions.

Mitigation: Automated Dynamic Slippage & Spread Widening. High-volume trades face automated slippage on bonding curves, while isolated margin limits (L 
p
​
 ) enforce instant liquidation when collateral thresholds are breached.  
ODT
+ 3

+ 2

PostgreSQL DDL Schema (supabase_schema.sql)
SQL
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "uuid-ossp";

DO $$ BEGIN
    CREATE TYPE combat_sport AS ENUM ('mma', 'boxing', 'muay_thai');
    CREATE TYPE position_status AS ENUM ('open', 'closed', 'liquidated');
EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN null; END $$;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.events (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    external_id TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    sport combat_sport NOT NULL,
    fighter_a TEXT NOT NULL,
    fighter_b TEXT NOT NULL,
    weight_class TEXT NOT NULL,
    start_time TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE NOT NULL,
    mma_metrics JSONB DEFAULT '{"td_defense_a": 0.75, "cage_control_a": 0.55}'::jsonb,
    boxing_metrics JSONB DEFAULT '{"punch_efficiency_a": 0.42, "chin_decay_b": 0.80}'::jsonb,
    muay_thai_metrics JSONB DEFAULT '{"clinch_efficiency_a": 0.88, "small_glove": 1.35}'::jsonb,
    created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc'::text, NOW()) NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.positions (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    user_id UUID REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE NOT NULL,
    event_id UUID REFERENCES public.events(id) ON DELETE CASCADE NOT NULL,
    market_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    entry_odds NUMERIC(8, 3) NOT NULL,
    stake NUMERIC(10, 2) NOT NULL CHECK (stake > 0),
    leverage INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (leverage >= 1 AND leverage <= 20),
    liquidation_price NUMERIC(8, 3) NOT NULL,
    status position_status DEFAULT 'open' NOT NULL,
    pnl NUMERIC(10, 2) DEFAULT 0.00,
    created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT TIMEZONE('utc'::text, NOW()) NOT NULL
);

ALTER TABLE public.events ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE public.positions ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY "Public events read" ON public.events FOR SELECT USING (true);
CREATE POLICY "User positions read" ON public.positions FOR SELECT USING (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE POLICY "User positions insert" ON public.positions FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);

ALTER PUBLICATION supabase_realtime ADD TABLE public.positions;
Python Master Backend Engine (backend/app/services/quant_engine.py)
Python
import numpy as np
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
from typing import List, Dict, Optional

class AMMPoolState(BaseModel):
    pool_id: str
    reserve_a: float = Field(..., gt=0)
    reserve_b: float = Field(..., gt=0)
    base_spread: float = Field(default=0.02, ge=0.005, le=0.10)

class AMMTradeResult(BaseModel):
    new_reserve_a: float
    new_reserve_b: float
    effective_odds: float
    applied_spread: float
    slippage_pct: float

class GRNTMasterEngine:
    def __init__(self, sim_runs: int = 10000):
        self.sim_runs = sim_runs

    def calculate_dynamic_spread(self, base_spread: float, volatility_index: float) -> float:
        return round(min(base_spread * (1.0 + 0.5 * (volatility_index - 1.0)), 0.06), 4)

    def execute_amm_buy(self, pool: AMMPoolState, stake: float, volatility: float = 1.0) -> AMMTradeResult:
        k = pool.reserve_a * pool.reserve_b
        spot_price = pool.reserve_b / pool.reserve_a
        new_reserve_a = pool.reserve_a + stake
        new_reserve_b = k / new_reserve_a
        tokens_out = pool.reserve_b - new_reserve_b
        exec_price = stake / tokens_out if tokens_out > 0 else spot_price
        
        slippage = abs(exec_price - spot_price) / spot_price
        spread = self.calculate_dynamic_spread(pool.base_spread, volatility)
        raw_odds = 1.0 / exec_price if exec_price > 0 else 1.0
        effective_odds = round(raw_odds * (1.0 - spread), 3)

        return AMMTradeResult(
            new_reserve_a=round(new_reserve_a, 2),
            new_reserve_b=round(new_reserve_b, 2),
            effective_odds=max(effective_odds, 1.01),
            applied_spread=spread,
            slippage_pct=round(slippage * 100, 2)
        )

    def evaluate_quant_ev(self, sport: str, metrics: Dict[str, float], decimal_odds: float) -> Dict[str, float]:
        base_p = 0.50
        if sport == 'mma':
            base_p += (metrics.get('td_defense_a', 0.5) * 0.15) + (metrics.get('cage_control_a', 0.5) * 0.10)
        elif sport == 'boxing':
            base_p += (metrics.get('punch_efficiency_a', 0.5) * 0.20) + (metrics.get('chin_decay_b', 0.5) * 0.15)
        elif sport == 'muay_thai':
            base_p += (metrics.get('clinch_efficiency_a', 0.5) * 0.25) * (1.1 if metrics.get('small_glove', 1.0) > 1.2 else 1.0)
        
        true_prob = float(round(np.sum(np.random.normal(np.clip(base_p, 0.05, 0.95), 0.04, self.sim_runs) >= 0.5) / self.sim_runs, 4))
        ev_margin = round((true_prob * decimal_odds) - 1.0, 4)
        
        b = decimal_odds - 1.0
        full_kelly = ((b * true_prob) - (1.0 - true_prob)) / b if b > 0 else 0
        kelly_pct = float(round(max(0.0, min(full_kelly * 0.25, 0.10)), 4))

        return {
            "implied_prob": round(1.0 / decimal_odds, 4),
            "true_prob": true_prob,
            "ev_margin": ev_margin,
            "kelly_pct": kelly_pct,
            "is_hot_signal": ev_margin > 0.05
        }
Frontend Zustand State Manager (frontend/src/store/useTradeSlipStore.ts)
TypeScript
import { create } from 'zustand';

export interface ActiveTradeDraft {
  eventId: string;
  marketName: string;
  entryOdds: number;
  stake: number;
  leverage: number; // 1x - 20x
  direction: 'LONG' | 'SHORT';
}

interface TradeSlipStore {
  draft: ActiveTradeDraft | null;
  setDraft: (draft: Partial<ActiveTradeDraft>) => void;
  setLeverage: (leverage: number) => void;
  setStake: (stake: number) => void;
  getRequiredMargin: () => number;
  getLiquidationOdds: () => number;
  getVaultHedgeCost: () => number;
  reset: () => void;
}

export const useTradeSlipStore = create<TradeSlipStore>((set, get) => ({
  draft: null,

  setDraft: (incoming) =>
    set((state) => ({
      draft: state.draft
        ? { ...state.draft, ...incoming }
        : {
            eventId: incoming.eventId || '',
            marketName: incoming.marketName || '',
            entryOdds: incoming.entryOdds || 2.0,
            stake: incoming.stake || 100,
            leverage: incoming.leverage || 1,
            direction: incoming.direction || 'LONG',
          },
    })),

  setLeverage: (leverage) =>
    set((state) => ({
      draft: state.draft
        ? { ...state.draft, leverage: Math.min(Math.max(leverage, 1), 20) }
        : null,
    })),

  setStake: (stake) =>
    set((state) => ({
      draft: state.draft ? { ...state.draft, stake: Math.max(stake, 1) } : null,
    })),

  getRequiredMargin: () => {
    const { draft } = get();
    if (!draft) return 0;
    return Number((draft.stake / draft.leverage).toFixed(2));
  },

  getLiquidationOdds: () => {
    const { draft } = get();
    if (!draft || draft.leverage <= 1) return 0;
    const dropFactor = 1 - 1 / draft.leverage;
    return Number((draft.entryOdds * dropFactor).toFixed(3));
  },

  getVaultHedgeCost: () => {
    const { draft } = get();
    if (!draft) return 0;
    return Number(((draft.stake / draft.leverage) * 0.05).toFixed(2));
  },

  reset: () => set({ draft: null }),
}));
Dynamic Heatmap UI Canvas Component (frontend/src/components/DynamicHeatmapCanvas.tsx)
TypeScript
'use client';

import React, { useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useTradeSlipStore } from '@/store/useTradeSlipStore';

export const DynamicHeatmapCanvas: React.FC<{ currentOdds: number }> = ({ currentOdds }) => {
  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);
  const { draft, getLiquidationOdds } = useTradeSlipStore();

  useEffect(() => {
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    if (!canvas || !draft) return;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) return;

    const width = canvas.width;
    const height = canvas.height;
    const liqOdds = getLiquidationOdds();

    const distanceToLiq = draft.leverage > 1 
      ? Math.max(0, (currentOdds - liqOdds) / (draft.entryOdds - liqOdds))
      : 1.0;

    const gradient = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, width, 0);
    if (distanceToLiq < 0.25) {
      gradient.addColorStop(0, '#FF0055');
      gradient.addColorStop(1, '#FF3300');
    } else {
      gradient.addColorStop(0, '#00FF66');
      gradient.addColorStop(0.5, '#FFCC00');
      gradient.addColorStop(1, '#FF0055');
    }

    ctx.fillStyle = gradient;
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);

    if (draft.leverage > 1) {
      const lineX = Math.min(Math.max((liqOdds / draft.entryOdds) * width, 10), width - 10);
      ctx.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF';
      ctx.fillRect(lineX - 2, 0, 4, height);
      ctx.font = 'bold 12px monospace';
      ctx.fillText(`MARGIN CALL: ${liqOdds}`, lineX + 6, 20);
    }
  }, [draft, currentOdds, getLiquidationOdds]);

  if (!draft) return <div className="text-gray-500 text-sm">Select market from Quant Screener...</div>;

  const liqOdds = getLiquidationOdds();
  const isAnxietyZone = draft.leverage > 1 && (currentOdds - liqOdds) < 0.15;

  return (
    <div className={`p-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 border ${isAnxietyZone ? 'border-red-500 animate-pulse' : 'border-slate-800'}`}>
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-2">
        <span className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono">RISK HEATMAP (1x - 20x LEVERAGE)</span>
        {isAnxietyZone && <span className="text-xs text-red-500 font-bold animate-bounce">⚠️ MARGIN CALL ANXIETY DETECTED</span>}
      </div>
      <canvas ref={canvasRef} width={400} height={60} className="w-full rounded-lg h-15" />
    </div>
  );
};
Playwright E2E Test Suite (frontend/tests/e2e/leverage-slider.spec.ts)
TypeScript
import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';

test.describe('GRNT APEX Terminal - E2E Test Suite', () => {
  test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
    await page.goto('/');
    await page.waitForLoadState('networkidle');
  });

  test('Leverage slider move (1x -> 5x) updates Margin and Liquidation displays in <100ms', async ({ page }) => {
    const eventCard = page.locator('[data-testid="event-card"]').first();
    await expect(eventCard).toBeVisible();
    await eventCard.click();

    const leverageDisplay = page.locator('[data-testid="leverage-display"]');
    await expect(leverageDisplay).toHaveText('1x');

    const leverageSlider = page.locator('[data-testid="leverage-slider"] input[type="range"], [data-testid="leverage-slider"][role="slider"]');
    await leverageSlider.focus();

    const startTime = Date.now();
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      await page.keyboard.press('ArrowRight');
    }

    await expect(leverageDisplay).toHaveText('5x');
    const executionTime = Date.now() - startTime;
    expect(executionTime).toBeLessThan(100);

    const marginDisplay = page.locator('[data-testid="margin-display"]');
    await expect(marginDisplay).not.toHaveText('100.00 €');

    const liquidationDisplay = page.locator('[data-testid="liquidation-display"]');
    const updatedText = await liquidationDisplay.innerText();
    const val = parseFloat(updatedText.replace(/[^0-9.]/g, ''));
    expect(val).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });
});

The GRNT APEX Terminal provides a solution to the iGaming industry's most pressing challenges: product commoditization, shrinking margins, and balance-sheet exposure.

Here is the complete, interactive GRNT APEX Terminal Master Application designed as a single-page Next.js 14+ (React + Tailwind CSS) app ready for instant deployment on Vercel.

app/page.tsx (Copy & Paste directly into Next.js / Vercel)
TypeScript
'use client';

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef, useMemo } from 'react';

// Types
type TabType = 'calculator' | 'roadmap' | 'matrix' | 'gamification' | 'architecture';
type AmmMode = 'modeA' | 'modeB';

export default function GRNTMasterVercelApp() {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<TabType>('calculator');
  const [selectedPhase, setSelectedPhase] = useState<number>(1);
  const [ammMode, setAmmMode] = useState<AmmMode>('modeA');

  // Interactive Financial Model State
  const [activeUsers, setActiveUsers] = useState<number>(50000);
  const [avgDeposit, setAvgDeposit] = useState<number>(200);
  const [avgLeverage, setAvgLeverage] = useState<number>(3.5);
  const [activePartners, setActivePartners] = useState<number>(5);

  // Interactive Gamification Simulator State
  const [simLeverage, setSimLeverage] = useState<number>(5);
  const [simStake, setSimStake] = useState<number>(100);
  const [simOdds, setSimOdds] = useState<number>(2.5);

  // Dynamic Financial Calculations
  const calc = useMemo(() => {
    const userCollateralTotal = activeUsers * avgDeposit;
    const tradedVolume = userCollateralTotal * avgLeverage;
    const casinoGGR = tradedVolume * 0.165; // 16.5% Parlay Hold
    const casinoNGR = casinoGGR * 0.90;     // 10% Deductions
    const casinoNetRevenue = casinoNGR * 0.85;

    const grntSaaS = activePartners * 6000;
    const grntNGRShare = casinoNGR * 0.15; // 15% NGR Share
    const grntVolumeYield = tradedVolume * 0.015; // 1.50% combined yield
    const grntMonthlyTotal = grntSaaS + grntNGRShare + grntVolumeYield;
    const grntARR = grntMonthlyTotal * 12;

    return {
      userCollateralTotal,
      tradedVolume,
      casinoGGR,
      casinoNGR,
      casinoNetRevenue,
      grntSaaS,
      grntNGRShare,
      grntVolumeYield,
      grntMonthlyTotal,
      grntARR,
    };
  }, [activeUsers, avgDeposit, avgLeverage, activePartners]);

  // Derived Gamification Calculations
  const reqMargin = simStake / simLeverage;
  const liqOdds = simLeverage > 1 ? Number((simOdds * (1 - 1 / simLeverage)).toFixed(3)) : 0;
  const vaultHedgeCost = Number((reqMargin * 0.05).toFixed(2));

  // HTML5 Canvas Heatmap Ref
  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    if (!canvas) return;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) return;

    const width = canvas.width;
    const height = canvas.height;

    // Render Risk Gradient
    const gradient = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, width, 0);
    if (simLeverage >= 10) {
      gradient.addColorStop(0, '#00FF66');
      gradient.addColorStop(0.4, '#FFCC00');
      gradient.addColorStop(0.8, '#FF0055');
      gradient.addColorStop(1, '#990000');
    } else {
      gradient.addColorStop(0, '#00FF66');
      gradient.addColorStop(0.6, '#FFCC00');
      gradient.addColorStop(1, '#FF0055');
    }

    ctx.fillStyle = gradient;
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);

    // Draw Liquidation Marker
    if (simLeverage > 1) {
      const lineX = Math.min(Math.max((liqOdds / simOdds) * width, 20), width - 20);
      ctx.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF';
      ctx.fillRect(lineX - 2, 0, 4, height);

      ctx.font = 'bold 11px monospace';
      ctx.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF';
      ctx.fillText(`MARGIN CALL: ${liqOdds}`, lineX + 6, 20);
    }
  }, [simLeverage, simStake, simOdds, liqOdds]);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#0A0A0C] text-slate-100 font-sans selection:bg-emerald-500 selection:text-black">
      {/* TOP GLOW & STATUS BAR */}
      <div className="border-b border-slate-800/80 bg-slate-950/80 backdrop-blur-xl sticky top-0 z-50">
        <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 py-3 flex flex-wrap justify-between items-center gap-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="relative">
              <span className="h-3 w-3 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 block" />
              <span className="h-3 w-3 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 block absolute top-0 animate-ping opacity-75" />
            </div>
            <span className="font-black tracking-wider text-white text-lg">GRNT APEX TERMINAL</span>
            <span className="px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-mono font-bold bg-emerald-950 text-emerald-400 border border-emerald-800 rounded">
              v1.0-LTS VERIFIED
            </span>
          </div>

          {/* Quick Metrics Bar */}
          <div className="hidden lg:flex items-center gap-6 text-xs font-mono">
            <div>
              <span className="text-slate-500">HOLD LIFT:</span> <span className="text-emerald-400 font-bold">+230%[cite: 1, 2]</span>
            </div>
            <div>
              <span className="text-slate-500">SESSION TIME:</span> <span className="text-emerald-400 font-bold">15 MIN[cite: 1, 2]</span>
            </div>
            <div>
              <span className="text-slate-500">TASE RISK:</span> <span className="text-emerald-400 font-bold">0% (AMM)[cite: 1, 2]</span>
            </div>
            <div>
              <span className="text-slate-500">NPV BASE:</span> <span className="text-emerald-400 font-bold">€539.06M</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Navigation Pills */}
          <div className="flex gap-1 bg-slate-900/90 p-1 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 text-xs">
            {[
              { id: 'calculator', label: 'Scenario Builder' },
              { id: 'roadmap', label: '4-Phase Roadmap' },
              { id: 'matrix', label: 'CPO Solutions' },
              { id: 'gamification', label: 'Dopamine Engine' },
              { id: 'architecture', label: 'Tech Architecture' },
            ].map((tab) => (
              <button
                key={tab.id}
                onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.id as TabType)}
                className={`px-3 py-1.5 font-bold rounded-lg transition-all ${
                  activeTab === tab.id
                    ? 'bg-emerald-500 text-black shadow-lg shadow-emerald-500/20'
                    : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-slate-800/50'
                }`}
              >
                {tab.label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* HERO KEY PERFORMANCE BAND */}
      <section className="border-b border-slate-800 bg-gradient-to-b from-slate-950 to-[#0A0A0C] py-8">
        <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
          <div className="bg-slate-900/60 border border-slate-800/80 p-4 rounded-2xl backdrop-blur">
            <div className="text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-400">GGR REVENUE MULTIPLIER</div>
            <div className="text-3xl font-black text-white mt-1">11.6x Uplift[cite: 1, 2]</div>
            <p className="text-[10px] text-emerald-400 mt-1 font-mono">€100k → €1.15M/mo per 10k users[cite: 1, 2]</p>
          </div>
          <div className="bg-slate-900/60 border border-slate-800/80 p-4 rounded-2xl backdrop-blur">
            <div className="text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-400">AVERAGE HOLD MARGIN</div>
            <div className="text-3xl font-black text-emerald-400 mt-1">16.5% Hold[cite: 1, 2]</div>
            <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 mt-1 font-mono">Vs. 5.0% legacy single bets[cite: 1, 2]</p>
          </div>
          <div className="bg-slate-900/60 border border-slate-800/80 p-4 rounded-2xl backdrop-blur">
            <div className="text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-400">SESSION DURATION</div>
            <div className="text-3xl font-black text-white mt-1">15.0 Minutes[cite: 1, 2]</div>
            <p className="text-[10px] text-emerald-400 mt-1 font-mono">+1,760% over 45s legacy slips[cite: 1, 2]</p>
          </div>
          <div className="bg-slate-900/60 border border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-950/10 p-4 rounded-2xl backdrop-blur">
            <div className="text-[11px] font-mono text-emerald-400">ARR RUN-RATE (Y5 BASE)</div>
            <div className="text-3xl font-black text-emerald-400 mt-1">€180.0M ARR[cite: 2]</div>
            <p className="text-[10px] text-emerald-500 mt-1 font-mono">DCF Valuation: €539.06M NPV[cite: 2]</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* MAIN INTERACTIVE SECTION */}
      <main className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 py-10">
        {/* TAB 1: INTERACTIVE FINANCIAL SCENARIO BUILDER & DCF */}
        {activeTab === 'calculator' && (
          <div className="space-y-10">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-8">
              {/* Controls Column */}
              <div className="lg:col-span-4 bg-slate-900/80 border border-slate-800 p-6 rounded-3xl space-y-6">
                <div>
                  <h2 className="text-lg font-black text-white flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="text-emerald-400 font-mono">01.</span> Scenario Controls
                  </h2>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1">Adjust parameters to simulate live P&L impact[cite: 2].</p>
                </div>

                <div className="space-y-5 text-xs font-mono">
                  <div>
                    <div className="flex justify-between mb-2">
                      <span className="text-slate-400">Active Monthly Users:</span>
                      <span className="text-emerald-400 font-bold">{activeUsers.toLocaleString()}</span>
                    </div>
                    <input
                      type="range"
                      min={1000}
                      max={200000}
                      step={1000}
                      value={activeUsers}
                      onChange={(e) => setActiveUsers(Number(e.target.value))}
                      className="w-full accent-emerald-500 bg-slate-800 h-2 rounded-lg cursor-pointer"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <div className="flex justify-between mb-2">
                      <span className="text-slate-400">Avg Deposit / User (€/mo):</span>
                      <span className="text-emerald-400 font-bold">€{avgDeposit}</span>
                    </div>
                    <input
                      type="range"
                      min={50}
                      max={1000}
                      step={10}

                <pre className="p-4 bg-slate-950 rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 text-[11px] font-mono text-emerald-400 overflow-x-auto">
{`grnt-apex-monorepo/
├── backend/
│   ├── app/
│   │   ├── api/                  # FastAPI routes (/events, /positions)
│   │   ├── services/
│   │   │   ├── quant_engine.py   # EV, Kelly & Sport Metrics Engine
│   │   │   ├── amm_engine.py     # AMM x*y=k & Dynamic Spread Engine
│   │   │   ├── recycler.py       # Capital Velocity & Liquidation Engine
│   │   │   └── counter.py        # House Counter-Portfolio Analytics
│   │   └── main.py               # FastAPI Entrypoint
├── frontend/
│   ├── src/
│   │   ├── app/                  # Next.js 14 App Router
│   │   ├── components/
│   │   │   ├── dashboard/        # Screener, TradeSlip, Heatmap
│   │   │   └── social/           # Realtime Copy-Trading Feed
│   │   ├── store/
│   │   │   └── useTradeSlipStore.ts # Zustand 1x-20x Leverage Store
│   │   └── lib/                  # Smart Router & Supabase client
│   └── tests/e2e/                # Playwright E2E spec`}
                </pre>
              </div>

                <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-white font-mono">AMM ENGINE CODE SNIPPET (`amm_engine.py`)[cite: 2]</h3>
                <pre className="p-4 bg-slate-950 rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-300 overflow-x-auto">
{`class GRNTAMMEngine:
    def calculate_dynamic_spread(self, base_spread: float, volatility: float) -> float:
        # Expands spread up to 6% during live fight panic
        return round(min(base_spread * (1.0 + 0.5 * (volatility - 1.0)), 0.06), 4)

    def execute_buy(self, pool: AMMPoolState, stake: float, volatility: float = 1.0):
        k = pool.reserve_a * pool.reserve_b
        spot_price = pool.reserve_b / pool.reserve_a
        new_reserve_a = pool.reserve_a + stake
        new_reserve_b = k / new_reserve_a
        tokens_out = pool.reserve_b - new_reserve_b
        exec_price = stake / tokens_out if tokens_out > 0 else spot_price
        
        spread = self.calculate_dynamic_spread(pool.base_spread, volatility)
        effective_odds = round((1.0 / exec_price) * (1.0 - spread), 3)
        return AMMTradeResult(new_reserve_a, new_reserve_b, effective_odds, spread)`}
                </pre>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </main>

      {/* FOOTER */}
      <footer className="border-t border-slate-800/80 bg-slate-950 mt-16 py-8 text-xs font-mono text-slate-500">
        <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-center gap-4">
          <div>
            © 2026 GRNT Engine Technologies Ltd. All Rights Reserved[cite: 1, 2].
          </div>

            <span>MGA B2C Type 2 Architecture[cite: 2]</span>
            <span>Sub-100ms WebSocket Telemetry[cite: 1, 2]</span>
            <span>Switchable Dual-Mode AMM[cite: 1]</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </footer>
    </div>
  );
}
How to Deploy this App to Vercel in 60 Seconds:
Create Next.js App:

Bash
npx create-next-app@latest grnt-apex-terminal --typescript --tailwind --app
cd grnt-apex-terminal
Replace app/page.tsx:
Copy and paste the code block above into app/page.tsx[cite: 2].

Deploy to Vercel:

Bash
npx vercel
Or push to GitHub/GitLab and import directly into the Vercel Dashboard.

Key Highlights of this Interactive Vercel Build:
Dedicated Room for Each Phase: The 4-Phase Roadmap tab includes expandable, high-impact cards with specific room for Phase 1 (Aggregator/Sandbox), Phase 2 (B2B SaaS), Phase 3 (MGA Type 2 AMM Pool), and Phase 4 (Media & Web3 Ingress)[cite: 1, 2].

Interactive Financial Scenario Builder: Real-time slider controls recalculate Casino P&L vs. GRNT Revenue Streams, updated for DCF NPV models (€539.06M base valuation)[cite: 2].

Dual-Mode AMM Compliance Switcher: Allows CPOs and investors to toggle between Mode A (Off-Tase Brokerage) and Mode B (Casino Regulated Vault)[cite: 1].

Live Gamification Simulator: Features an interactive 1x–20x leverage slider, dynamic HTML5 canvas risk heatmap, heartbeat haptics, and a sub-200ms WebSocket copy-trading feed simulator[cite: 1, 2].

Full Developer Operating Specifications: Includes monorepo structures, PostgreSQL DDL scripts, Python FastAPI microservices, Zustand stores, and Playwright E2E assertion tests[cite: 2].
